## FINGERPRINTS (each seat round is one seat for one 1,000 hand round)
FG: Gherkers: at least 3 opens, 90% or more of them exactly 3.5bb, and either a non all-in postflop bet of 1.08 to 1.12 pot or a 3-bet of 3.78x to 3.82x the open.
FEE: Equity Engine: at least 5 opens, 90% or more exactly 3.5bb; at least 3 non all-in postflop bets, 90% or more of them at 0.45, 0.50, 0.80 or 1.05 pot (within 0.015) with at least one at 0.80; every 3-bet between 2.9x and 3.45x the open.
FA: Attention Is All In: at least 5 opens, 90% or more of them at 2, 2.5, 3 or 4bb, with a 4bb or a 2bb open seen; at least 3 non all-in postflop bets, half or more at 0.31 to 0.35 pot.
FTMM: The Market Makers: 90% of opens 2.5bb (>=5) and 80% of postflop bets at 0.5, 0.6, 0.7 or 1.0 pot (>=3).
FAAST: AAST: 90% of opens 3bb (>=5) and 70% of postflop bets at 1.1, 0.8, 0.7 or 0.33 pot (>=3).
FW: WBot: 90% of opens at 2.8, 3.3, 3.5 or 4bb (>=5) and 40% of postflop bets at 0.15 pot (>=3).
FK: Kojack: 90% of opens 2.5 or 3bb (>=5) and 60% of postflop bets at 0.40, 0.45, 0.55 or 0.72 pot (>=3).
FANK: Anker Batteries: 90% of opens 2.5bb (>=5) and 70% of postflop bets at 0.4, 0.62, 0.7, 1.1 or 2.0 pot (>=3).
